# wzSearch – data-quality report

Generated `2026-11-12T06:55:36Z` by `pipeline/build.py` from the district's [source index](https://wawer.um.warszawa.pl/dokumenty-budowlane), the [BAiPP lists](https://architektura.um.warszawa.pl/postepowania-i-decyzje) and GUNB's [RWDZ register](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pobranie.html). Regenerated on every build; do not edit.

## Totals

* Records per dataset: Dzielnica 5727, BAiPP (miasto) 51, GUNB RWDZ 6344 – `records.json` 5778, `permits.json` 6344.
* Monthly pages: **1** (– → –); source files: **47** (47 with rows, 0 empty templates, 0 unreadable/missing).

* Building permits and notifications (RWDZ): **6344** (Pozwolenie 5755, Zgłoszenie 589).

## Declared vs parsed counts

Each monthly page states how many entries each list has ("3 pozycje"). `parsed` counts the data rows read from the files first published on that page (a leftover link to an earlier month's file is not counted twice).

| Kind | Declared (blocks with a number) | Parsed in those blocks | Parsed in blocks without a number | Parsed rows | Duplicates merged | Records |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| WZ | 0 | 0 | 3146 | 2728 | 2 | 2726 |
| CP | 0 | 0 | 609 | 609 | 66 | 543 |
| Wszczęcie | 0 | 0 | 2069 | 2487 | 29 | 2458 |

All blocks with a declared count match the parsed files.

## Completeness: gaps in decision numbering

WZ and CP decisions are numbered consecutively per year, so a number that never appears in any file is a decision that was not published in these lists (or a refusal/discontinuation the office does not list). Numbering restarts every year at 1; in the first year only numbers after the lowest one seen count, because the publication started in August 2020. (District numbering only: BAiPP numbers its decisions city-wide.)

| Kind | Year | Published | Range seen | Missing | Missing numbers |
|---|---|---:|---|---:|---|

## GUNB RWDZ (building permits and notifications)

GUNB publishes its register of building-permit decisions and notifications (*Rejestr wniosków, decyzji i zgłoszeń*, CC0 1.0) as three ZIP files rebuilt every night. Every case with a parcel in Praga-Południe (cadastral unit `146507_8`) is copied byte for byte into a committed extract (`data/raw/rwdz/`, mirrored to `site/sources/rwdz/`) and becomes one record in `permits.json`; the snapshot cited is the one the extract was taken from (a new GUNB file that changes nothing in the district is not adopted).

| File | Kind | Snapshot (Last-Modified) | Rows in the file | Praga-Południe rows | Records | Extract |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| [wynik_mazowieckie.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_mazowieckie.zip) | Pozwolenie | 2026-10-26T22:35:07Z | 695711 | 10687 | 5755 | [`sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-wnioski.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-wnioski.csv) |
| [wynik_zgloszenia_2016_2021.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_zgloszenia_2016_2021.zip) | Zgłoszenie | 2026-10-26T22:36:03Z | 829354 | 789 | 334 | [`sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021.csv) |
| [wynik_zgloszenia_2022_up.zip](https://wyszukiwarka.gunb.gov.pl/pliki_pobranie/wynik_zgloszenia_2022_up.zip) | Zgłoszenie | 2026-10-26T22:36:07Z | 939616 | 1038 | 255 | [`sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2022.csv`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/sources/d/wawer/rwdz/rwdz-zgloszenia-2022.csv) |

* `rwdz-wnioski`: kept – empty 6, invalid 11, typo 26; not kept – none.
* `rwdz-zgloszenia-2016-2021`: kept – typo 1; not kept – none.
* `rwdz-zgloszenia-2022`: kept – none; not kept – none.
* Records with a corrected unit: 6 ([`pb-st-mz-ww-2018-33573`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2018-33573?dz=wawer), [`pb-st-mz-ww-2018-25713`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2018-25713?dz=wawer), [`pb-st-mz-ww-2018-7775`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2018-7775?dz=wawer), [`pb-st-mz-ww-2018-31964`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2018-31964?dz=wawer), [`pb-st-mz-ww-2017-22903`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2017-22903?dz=wawer), [`pb-st-mz-ww-2017-22789`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/pb-st-mz-ww-2017-22789?dz=wawer)).

## Related records across datasets

`related` links records that share a cadastral parcel or a street + house number, across all three datasets. A shared parcel is not necessarily the same project (a large housing-estate parcel carries many unrelated renovations).

* Dzielnica: 2567 of 5727 records relate to at least one permit or notification.
* BAiPP (miasto): 9 of 51 records relate to at least one permit or notification.
* GUNB RWDZ: 1824 of 6344 permits/notifications relate to a WZ/ULICP record.
* WZ decisions (not refused/discontinued) followed by a building permit on the same parcel: 838 of 2026 (median 404 days to the first such permit).
* CP decisions (not refused/discontinued) followed by a building permit on the same parcel: 332 of 533 (median 220 days to the first such permit).
* Most related: [`cp-waw-2021-23`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/cp-waw-2021-23?dz=wawer) (86), [`ws-waw-6733-2020-141`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-waw-6733-2020-141?dz=wawer) (86), [`cp-waw-2020-138`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/cp-waw-2020-138?dz=wawer) (83), [`cp-waw-2024-19`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/cp-waw-2024-19?dz=wawer) (58), [`ws-waw-6733-2023-59`](https://3-n.github.io/wzsearch/#/wpis/ws-waw-6733-2023-59?dz=wawer) (58).
